(PID.TID 0000.0001) > &EXF_NML_01

(PID.TID 0000.0001) > useExfYearlyFields = .FALSE.,
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#-- Task 6 fix: pkg/exf/exf_check_range.F hard-STOPs on hflux values
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   above a hardcoded 1600 W/m^2 sanity cap (observed up to ~2300
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   W/m^2 here) and on tiny (~1.e-8, essentially rounding noise)
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   negative precip values. Both are expected consequences of this
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   plan's documented, non-scientific LLC90->LLC30 coarsening (monthly-
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   mean + naive 3x3 block-average forcing, no vector-field rotation --
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   see preprocess/README.md and the plan's Non-Goals: "no scientific
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   validation of circulation" is explicitly out of scope). exf_check_
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   range.F's own WARNING text names this exact flag as the sanctioned
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   escape hatch ("If you think these values are OK then set
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   useExfCheckRange=.FALSE."); using it here rather than hand-tuning
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   the coarsened forcing to pass an arbitrary sanity threshold.
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   NOTE: this flag is global -- it also suppresses exf_check_range.F's
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   ustress/vstress range checks, not just hflux/precip, which removes an
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   independent signal that could otherwise catch a future unit/sign error
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   in wind-stress coarsening. There is no narrower per-field escape hatch
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   in pkg/exf, so this is an accepted trade-off (see README.md's Non-Goals
(PID.TID 0000.0001) >#   section), not an oversight.
(PID.TID 0000.0001) > useExfCheckRange   = .FALSE.,
